> PROTOCOL 16 referred to in paragraph 2 of Article 3/

> ABOLITION OF CUSTOMS DUTIES BETWEEN THE REPUBLIC OF POLAND ON THE ONE SIDE AND ROMANIA ON THE OTHER SIDE

> 1. As from the date of application of this Agreement all industrial products within the meaning of Article 2 of this Agreement, other than those listed in Annexes A to L to this Protecol and originating in the Republic of Poland or in Romania are subject to zero customs duties when imported to the Republic of Poland or to Romania.

> 2. Customs duties on imports to the Republic of Poland of industrial products originating in Romania.

> 2.1. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex A to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 70% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 35% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 2.2. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex B to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 75% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 60% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to 45% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to 30% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> [Strona 141 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> 2.3. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex C to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to five-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to four-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to three-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to two-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - to one-seventh of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2002 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 2.4. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex D to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to six-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to five-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to four-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to three-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - to two-sevenths of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2002 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 2.5. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex E to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on January 1, 1998 - to 12% ad valorem, - on January 1, 1999 - to 9% ad valorem, - on January 1, 2000 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 2.6. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex F to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 25% ad valorem, - on January 1, 1998 - to 21% ad valorem, - on January I, 1999 - to 17% ad valorem, - on January 1, 2000 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 2.7. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex G to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 9% ad valorem, - on January 1, 1999 - to 6% ad valorem, - on January 1, 2000 - to 3% ad valorem, - on January 1, 2001 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 3. Customs duties on imports to Romania of industrial products originating in the Republic of Poland.

> 3.1. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Poland, listed in Annex H to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 70% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 35% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> 3.2. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Poland, listed in Annex J to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 75% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 60% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to 45% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to 30% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - the remaining duties shall be abolished.

> PROTOCOL 17 /referred to in paragraph 2 of Article 3/

> ABOLITION OF CUSTOMS DUTIES BETWEEN THE REPUBLIC OF SLOVENIA ON THE ONE SIDE AND ROMANIA ON THE OTHER SIDE

> \|. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia listed in Annex A to this Protocol shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 75% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 50% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to 25% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 2. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia listed in Annex B to this Protocol shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 70% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 60% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to 40% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - to 20% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2002 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 3. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia listed in Annex C to this Protocol shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 80% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 60% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to 40% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - to 20% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2002 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> [Strona 208 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> 4. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia not listed in Annexes A, B and C to this Protocol shall be abolished on the date of application of this Agreement.

> 5. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania listed in Annex D to this Protocol shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 75% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 50% of the basic duty, - on January I, 1999 - to 25% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 6. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania listed in Annex E to this Protocol shall be progressively reduced in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of the Agreement - to 80% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 60% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - to 40% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2000 - to 20% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 2001 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 7. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania not listed in Annexes D and E to this Protocol shall be abolished on the date of application of this Agreement.

> [Strona 209 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> PROTOCOL 18 /referred to in Article 12/

> EXCHANGE OF AGRICULTURAL CONCESSIONS BETWEEN THE CZECH REPUBLIC AND THE SLOVAK REPUBLIC ON TAE ONE SIDE AND ROMANIA ON THE OTHER SIDE

> 1. Custonis duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic and customs duties on imports applicable in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic to products originating in Romania listed in Annex A to this Protocol shall be abolished from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In those cases the customs duties shall be progressively abolished in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement —- to 50% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, - on January I, 1999 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 2. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic and customs duties on imports applicable in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic to products originating in Romania listed in Annex B to this Protocol shall be reduced to the level set out in this Annex as from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In those cases the customs duties shall be progressively reduced to the level set out in this Annex in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement - to 50% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B,

> \- on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B, - on January 1, 1999 - to the level set out in Annex B.

> [Strona 221 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> 3. Customs duttes on imports applicable in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic to products originating in Romania listed in Annex C to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 4. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Czech Republic and the Slovak Republic listed in Annex D to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 5. Basic duties agreed under this Protocol are the Most Favoured Nation duty rates applied at the time of actual importation. Customs duties include ad valorem and specific customs duties and product specific levies.

> PROTOCOL 20 /referred to in Article 12/

> EXCHANGE OF AGRICULTURAL CONCESSIONS BETWEEN THE REPUBLIC OF POLAND ON THE ONE SIDE AND ROMANIA ON THE OTHER SIDE

> 1. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Poland and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania listed in Annex A to this Protocol shall be abolished from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In those cases the customs duties shall be progressively abolished in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement -to 50% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 2. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Poland and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania listed in Annex B to this Protocol shall be reduced to the level set out in this Annex as from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In those cases the customs duties shall be progressively reduced to the level set out in this Annex in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application ofthis Agreement —- to 50% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B,

> \- on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B, - on January I, 1999 - to the level set out in Annex B.

> [Strona 237 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> 3. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Poland to products originating in Romania listed in Annex C to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 4. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Poland listed in Annex D to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 5. Basic duties agreed under this Protocol are the Most Favoured Nation duty rates applied at the time of actual importation. Customs duties include ad valorem and specific customs duties and product specific levies.

> PROTOCOL 21

> /referred to in Article 12/

> EXCHANGE OF AGRICULTURAL CONCESSIONS BETWEEN THE REPUBLIC OF SLOVENIA ON THE ONE SIDE AND ROMANIA ON THE OTHER SIDE

> 1. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania listed in Annex A to this Protocol shall be abolished from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In the case of items marked by double asterisk, the customs duties shall be progressively abolished by both Parties in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement - to 50% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, - on January 1, 1999 - the remaining duties shall be eliminated.

> 2. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex Al to this Protocol, shall be abolished by both Parties from the date of application of this Agreement within the limits of quotas specified in Annex Al. The quotas in Annex Al shall be abolished at the time as it will be established between the Republic of Slovenia and the other CEFTA countries.

> 3. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania listed in Annex B to this Protocol shall be reduced to the level set out in this Annex as from the date of application of this Agreement, with the exception of the items marked by asterisk. In the case of items marked by double asterisk, the customs duties shall be progressively reduced by both Parties to the level set out in this Annex in accordance with the following timetable:

> [Strona 245 PDF nie ma czytelnej warstwy tekstowej. Tekst poniżej odczytał OCR (tesseract 5.5.0, pol+eng). Może zawierać błędy i pomija grafikę. Wiążący jest PDF.]

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement ' - to 50% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B,

> \- on January 1, 1998 - to 30% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B, - on January 1, 1999 - to the level set out in Annex B.

> 4. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia and customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products originating in Romania, listed in Annex Bl to this Protocol, shall be progressively reduced by both Parties within the limits of quotas specified in Annex Bl, in accordance with the following timetable:

> \- on the date of application of this Agreement - to 45% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B, - on January 1, 1998 - to 40% of the basic duty, but maximum to the level set out in Annex B, - on January 1, 1999 - to the level set out in Annex B.

> The quotas in Annex Bl shall be abolished at the time as it will be established between the Republic of Slovenia and the other CEFTA countries.

> 5. Customs duties on imports applicable in Romania to products originating in the Republic of Slovenia listed in Annex C to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 6. Customs duties on imports applicable in the Republic of Slovenia to products Originating in Romania listed in Annex D to this Protocol shall be reduced, without quantitative limits or within the quotas specified in this Annex, to the level set out in this Annex from the date of application of this Agreement.

> 7.Customs duties referred to in this Protocol include ad valorem and specific customs duties and product specific levies. Basic duties agreed under this Protocol are the Most Favoured Nation duty rates applied at the time of actual importation.

Po zapoznaniu się z powyższą umową, w imieniu Rzeczypospolitej Polskiej oświadczam, że: — została ona uznana za słuszną zarówno w całości, jak i każde z postanowień w niej zawartych, — jest przyjęta, ratyfikowania i potwierdzona, — będzie niezmiennie zachowywana.

Na dowód czego wydany został akt niniejszy, opatrzony pieczęcią Rzeczypospolitej Polskiej.

Dano w Warszawie dnia 30 kwietnia 2002 r.

*Prezydent Rzeczypospolitej Polskiej: A. Kwaśniewski*

L.S. Prezes Rady Ministrów: L. Miller

* Stosowane przez Republikę Słowacką jedynie wobec produktów zawierających substancje niszczące warstwę ozonową Ziemi.
